### 9 November: D14 private pickup core (not a completed handover workflow)

Prepared the reusable cryptographic and quantity rules for the remaining D14
journey. Each generated secret has 256 random bits; HMAC purpose and actual order
UUID bind its hash. Validation rejects expired, future-issued, revoked, consumed,
wrong-order, malformed and incorrect secrets with one safe error. Raw pickupCode
fields are redacted at request/response log paths. The code accepts an explicit
future expiry with a seven-day security ceiling chosen in engineering; this is not
an owner-approved business hold or a selected operational token lifetime.

Partial plans validate actual order lines against grouped physical handover totals,
reject over-collection and duplicate/unknown lines, and leave exact remainders. They
never infer handover from payment/stock/READY status. Actual authorisation, current

transactional consumption, handover audit, customer/guest/staff UI and concurrent
acceptance remain to be connected. No endpoint or capability was enabled. The pure
core cannot claim atomicity or physical acceptance by itself. Final isolated acceptance passed 63/63 in four suites, including the final

### 1 November 2026: preserve concern identity on booking retries

Booking creation now compares the immutable saved customer concern when replaying
an existing request key. Older request fingerprints did not include this field;
changing it could silently return the previous booking. A changed concern now gets
409 with review guidance. Existing fingerprints remain compatible; identical retries
still return the original booking after staff classification and confirmation.

of disposable data removed, logs retained under TEMP/aat-concern-retry-
d2b81b21e0e64e669f92bb83acd4e018. C: free 26.47 GiB after cleanup.

### 30 October 2026: Allie candidate ranking before limits

Found a recall defect: public service/product/vehicle queries selected twelve matches
by ID before downstream relevance ranking. With many weak matches, the requested
record could never reach Allie. Added parameterised database candidate ranking using
title/name/brand/make/model term coverage before description coverage, with stable
ID tie-breaking and twelve IDs per catalogue. The bounded eight-term input uses
literal substring matching, not SQL patterns or user-supplied SQL.

The SQL prefilter mirrors current public visibility; canonical ORM visibility filters
and public projections are reapplied when loading candidates. Existing follow-up
IDs, exact amounts, full faults/restrictions, four-source/8000-character context
budget, permission checks and invalidation remain intact. Empty/no-keyword requests
no longer retrieve twelve arbitrary services. Pipeline revision is public-knowledge-v3
to invalidate old conversation context on rollout. No new migration or dependency.

The new native test creates 753 public catalogue records, placing the relevant
service/product/vehicle listing after 250 weaker IDs in each catalogue. All three
rank first; full retrieval finds them within the 36-new-source cap, preserves price
and fault disclosure, returns no arbitrary result for a stopword-only query, and
excludes all three after unpublication. This is a bounded local relevance proof,
not a production latency/load benchmark. Whole-catalogue revision hashing remains
linear and production-volume performance still needs evidence.

Fresh aat_allie_ranking_fa1c0e7425_test at 127.0.0.1:49293 passed 80-migration replay,
no drift and db:check. All 18 native integration tests pass across ranking, canonical
knowledge and conversation security (20.55s). All 76 focused unit tests, backend
types, scoped lint, formatting and diff whitespace checks pass. Initial scale
fixture failed publication constraints; corrected timestamps and one vehicle per
live listing, without relaxing database rules. That failed run is not a pass.
No paid OpenAI call, application database write or deployment occurred. Both test

aat-allie-ranking-03f09291385842a283ef5c33741950c1 and
aat-allie-ranking-58028acd4fa24e1b987061f6dea45d1b.

### 30 October 2026: native customer-to-workforce booking journey

Added concern-booking-journey.test.ts using real HTTP middleware, sessions, CSRF,
idempotency and a fresh isolated PostgreSQL database. The test submits and replays
one customer request with an account vehicle and notes, rejects another customer's
read/alternative response, classifies through workforce assignment, proposes a time,
accepts as the original customer, reassigns for the new time and explicitly confirms
through the authorised workforce endpoint with synthetic approved capacity.

clears the old assignment and stays REQUESTED; only explicit staff confirmation
makes it CONFIRMED. Quote/deposit remain unknown/null, customer reschedule quota is
unchanged and no Paystack key, model call or real notification is required.

## Allie public service knowledge — 30 October 2026

Implemented canonical public Service retrieval in the existing Allie conversation
pipeline. Search reads only active services, uses the existing serviceSelect public
projection, bounds question terms to eight and new matches to twelve, and re-reads
up to twenty-four prior source IDs for bounded follow-ups. No catalogue write, URL
fetch, private record, stock claim or appointment availability inference is added.
Fixed prices retain integer minor-unit precision; quotations remain unknown. Complete
conditions are preserved; passages exceeding 6000 characters are omitted rather than
cropped. The established four-source/8000-character model budget remains intact.

Internal sources include the canonical record ID, public permission, record version,
content SHA-256, record update time and retrieval time. A database-computed SHA-256
of current public service fields supplies the conversation revision. Changes even
without a version bump, and unpublication, invalidate history through existing read,
in-flight permission checks, pre/post-provider validation and final commit checks.
The versioned canonical records remain the source of truth; no duplicated index or
new migration was introduced. Browser citation validation now permits only a service
UUID whose destination exactly matches its source, never a model-supplied URL.

actual-HTTP conversation security tests and new canonical service retrieval,

## Working agreement

The user requested on 22 October 2026 that we finish the site one item at a time,
remember the decisions and continue until the agreed scope is complete. Use this
file as the durable handoff. Read it before resuming completion work, update the
current item and evidence, and do not repeat settled approval questions.

Completion means the agreed customer and staff journeys are implemented, verified,
operationally owned and supported by approved business policies. Optional additions
are separate; a configuration flag or passing build alone does not prove completion.

## Constraints and settled decisions

- No new costs, subscriptions or purchases without the user's approval.
- Frontend remains on Vercel; backend/database/storage are on DigitalOcean.
- Public accounts and enquiries are authorised. Purchase enquiries replace checkout
  for now. Service visits and vehicle inspections require staff confirmation; they
  do not allocate appointments or hold vehicles.
- Paystack is not supplied. Paystack/Monnify remain disabled; do not enable full
  transaction mode to bypass policy or provider checks.
- Preserve all application data and follow AGENTS.md database safeguards. Database
  tests use newly isolated test databases only.
- No automatic deletion of company-controlled customer records. Company staff
  authorise disposal; the approved narrow provider-expiry exception still applies.
- Expanded accounts-and-enquiries privacy wording is approved. Public provider
  wording is "website hosting, cloud storage and email service providers".
- VAT instructions and ordinary retention periods are already approved; consult
  the policy record rather than requesting the same approvals again.
- Do not send external email or other messages without explicit authorisation.
- Do not store passwords, tokens, customer records or recovery secrets here.

## 10 November - Release gate clock-boundary fixture repair (pending CI)

Production publication remains held. Branch f5969c84 backend safety42718157628
failed four late-visit fixture setups (278 passed,4 failed,4 skipped). After23:00UTC,
its Date.now()+1h day selection froze JavaScript at tomorrow's noon. Synthetic
resource-policy effectiveAt was consequently in the future relative to PostgreSQL's
real clock, and the existing allocation trigger correctly rejected it.

Only the test fixture changes: choose the most recent past UTC noon, including
before noon UTC and near Lagos midnight. Keep all18 reschedule assertions,
allocation triggers, runtime source, privileges and migrations unchanged. No
production/application database is written, reset, repaired or migrated. The
isolated GitHub safety job must pass for the resulting exact master source,
alongside frontend and secret gates, before the combined public mobile release.

Follow-up d5d1ad22 safety42719066777 passed281 with one failure: the allocation
fix succeeded, but freezing a past day correctly failed the independent visit-
continuation guard requiring PostgreSQL's current Lagos date. The final fixture
creates its policies/resources on the real clock before freezing noon of the
current Lagos date. This preserves both guards and the full same-day assertion;
no runtime, migration, privilege or data policy changes. Re-run isolated CI.

445ef4a8 safety42719688021 reached allocation but correctly rejected the isolated
actor's one-hour session after advancing to the simulated visit time. The fixture
now creates fresh owner/customer test sessions at that simulated time using the
existing paymentTestSession helper, retaining MFA and expiry checks. This changes
only disposable isolated fixtures; no session/security runtime rule is relaxed.

The fixture policy clock is bounded by both realNow and the visit noon, so the
same repair also works after noon UTC when the simulated noon lies in the past.
Its visit date remains today's Lagos date, and new isolated sessions are created
at that visit time. Boundary arithmetic checks before noon, after noon and at
23:00UTC verify policyTime<=realNow, policyTime<=visitTime and matching Lagos day;
only actual-current-time isolated CI is database-backed evidence.
